nam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya neṟiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉi varalāṟṟu muṟiyāl
ārāykiṉṟavar, ivvaḷarcci kuṟittuc camaṇa muṉivar ceytuḷḷa toṇṭukaḷik kāṇātupōka
muṭiyātu. tamiḻ vaḷarccikkeṉac ciṟanta toṇṭu purintu tamiḻ makkaḷiviṭac camaṇa
muṉivarē tali ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu palarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar ceyta toṇṭiṉi oru
ciṟitu ārāyiṉ, avar toṇṭu muṟṟum tam camaya neṟiyiṉit tamiḻnāṭṭil
nilipeṟuvittaṟkuc ceyta camayat toṇṭākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, ataṉip pulappaṭukkum
kuṟippāl ikkaṭṭuri eḻutappeṟukiṉṟatu.
1. camaṇa muṉivar varalāṟu
camaṇa muṉivar eṉpavar ciṉa camaya voḻukkatti mēṟkoṇṭu, tuṟavikaḷāy, naṉñāṉam, naṟkāṭci,
nalloḻukkam ciṟantu, viṉittoṭarpu keṭumāṟu muyalum cāṉṟōrāvar. itu paṇṭik
kālattil vaṭanāṭṭil tōṉṟiya putuc camayam eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. camayak kaliyil
valluṉarākiya irātākiruṭṭiṉaṉ eṉṉum pērāciriyar, “iḵtu intiya nāṭṭiṟkē
urittākiya vitika camayattiliruntu putitāyk kiḷitta camayam" eṉkiṉṟār. ic camayam
vaṭanāṭṭil ciṉa camayam eṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭil camaṇa camayam eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ciṉaruḷ,
illaṟattōr cirāvakar eṉṟum, tuṟavu pūṇṭu iṉpattu viruppum, tuṉpattu veṟuppum
ilarāy iraṇṭaṉiyum oppa matittoḻukum cāṉṟōr camaṇar eṉavum kūṟappavar. ivarkaḷ, putta
camayatti nāṭeṅkum parapputaṟkuriya peru muyaṟciyiṉic ceyta pēraracap peruntakiyākiya
acōka maṉṉarālum naṉku matikkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. iccamaṇarkaḷ nam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuntu tam
camaya neṟiyiṉip parappiyataṉāl, ivarkaḷatu camayamākiya ciṉa camayam ivarkaḷ peyarāl camaṇa
camayam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
2. camaṇa muṉivar varaviṉkaṇ tamiḻ irunta nilimi
tamiḻnāṭṭil, ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷē vāḻntu vantaṉar; avarkaḷpāl taṉit tamiḻē
nilaviyiruntatu. piṉṉar, vaṭa nāṭṭu makkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntaṉar. avar vaḻaṅkiya
vaṭamoḻiyum avarōṭu pōntu tamiḻnāṭṭil iṭam peṟṟatu. vaṭavarum tamiḻarum vāḻkkiyil
oruṅku iyintu ulavat toṭaṅkiyatum, vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻiṭik kalantu vaḻaṅkap
peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. camaṇar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukunta kālattut tamiḻariṭi vaṭavar vaḻakkum,
vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkum nilaviyiruntaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmiyum, vaṭavar vaḻakkiṉiyum,
vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkiṉiyum mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukutaṟkaṇ pērārvamum, pērūkkamum koṇṭu iruntatu.
nam nāṭṭiṟkup putiyarāy, putu vaḻakkum, putu voḻukkum, putu moḻiyum uṭiyarāyp
pōnta mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl, nam nāṭṭavarkku avar moḻi vaḻakkiṉum,
naṭiyoḻukkiṉum vēṭkiyum viḻivum peruka, ataṉāl nam nāṭṭu moḻi nili
tāḻvuṟṟataṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamē, vaṭavar koṇarnta moḻi vaḻakkiṉiyum, naṭiyoḻukkiṉiyum kaṇṭa
tamiḻar avaṟṟiṉpāl eḻunta vēṭkiyāl, tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṟ karuttūṉṟātu tāḻntaṉar.
vaṭavaratu vitika camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. vaṭavaratu vaṭamoḻi uyarntōrkkallatu
uṇarttappaṭā teṉṉum vaṭavar vakutta muṟiyāl, ikkālattu āṅkilam pōlat tamiḻariṭam
mutaliṭam peṟātāyiṟṟu. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyit tamiḻoṭu viravip pēcum neṟiyiṉi maṭṭum
kiviṭātu pēṇivantaṉar. ataṉāl, vaṭacol viravāta taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ arukiṉa; vaṭacol
viraviya nūlkaḷ kūṭiya aḷaviṟ perukiṉa. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻoṭu naṭipeṟṟu
iyalutaṟku iṭam vakuttu amippavāyiṉa. “vaṭacoṟkiḷavi vaṭaveḻuttorīi eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta
collākummē” eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
ivvaṟu vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl, tamiḻar tam tāymoḻi vaḷarccikkaṇ ūkkam kuṟintiruppatiyum,
vaṭamoḻikkaṇ viruppum, vitika camayak katikaḷil vēṭkiyum mikkuṟṟiruppatiyum, camaṇa
muṉivar teḷiya vuṇarntaṉar. tamiḻar, vitika camayappaṟṟu mikuntu atupaṟṟiya katikaḷik
kēṭṭu, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka varum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷiyum katikaḷiyum
mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkut tāḻttaṉar. ivvāṟu tampāl vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum tamiḻar kūṭṭuṟavip
peṟutaṟkuriya neṟiyil camaṇa muṉivar muyalvārāyiṉar. tamiḻarkku vaṭanūṟ katikaḷil
viruppu mikkiruntamiyiṉ, tāmum vaṭanūṟkaḷiyum, vaṭanūl marapukaḷiyum tamiḻiṉkaṭ
koṇarumukattāl tamiḻar ātaravip peṟalāyiṉar. in neṟikkup peruṅ karuviyāka
viḷaṅkuvatu iḷañ ciṟārkkuk kalvi payiṟṟuvikkum perun toṇṭēyākum eṉṟu uṇarntaṉar.
ataṉāl tām taṅkiyirunta paḷḷikaḷil kūṭaṅkaḷ amittu, avaṟṟuḷ iruntu ciṟuvarkkuk
kalvik koṭi puriyalāyiṉar. annāḷ toṭṭē kalvi payilum iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ eṉa
vaḻaṅkappaṭuvavāyiṉa.
3. . tamiḻttoṇṭu
camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu tēvāra kālattukku muṉṉiyatum, piṉṉiyatum eṉa
iruvakippaṭum. tēvāra kālattum, ataṟku muṉṉarum irunta camaṇa muṉivar peruṅ
kalviyum, aṟam urikkum amitiyum nirampit tuṟavaṟamē cīritāka mēṟkoṇṭu iruntaṉar.
ivarkaḷ neṟiyiṉit kaṭintu vātupurinta civap periyōrkaḷāṉa ñāṉacampantar mutaliyōr
ivarkaḷiṉ peruṅ kalviyiṉiyum, aṟavuri kaḷiyum cuṭṭi irukkiṉṟaṉar. "amaṇarum
kuṇṭarum cākkiyarum nayamuka uriyiṉar", “otiyum kēṭṭum uṇar viṉiyilātār
uḷkalākātatōr iyalpiṉiyuṭiyār" eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ, aṟam uritta perumiyiṉi, “pāṭuṭik
kuṇṭar cākkiyar camaṇar payiltarum aṟavuri", “aṟaṅkāṭṭum amaṇarum cākkiyarum",
"kaṭumali uṭaluṭi amaṇarum kañciyuṇ cākkiyarum, iṭum aṟavuri" eṉa ñāṉacampantap
perumāṉ kūṟiyaruḷiyirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟāl, tēvāra kālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta camaṇa
muṉivar tam camaya aṟaṅkaḷi makkaḷukku uṇarttuvatē tamatu kaṭaṉākak koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu
teḷiya viḷaṅkukiṟatu. akkālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta tamiḻ nūlkaḷi nōkkiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ
camaṇa muṉivar ceytaṉavākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉa yāvum aṟanūlkaḷēyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. nālaṭiyār,
aṟaneṟiccāram, ciṟupañcamūlam mutaliya palavum aṟam kūṟuvaṉavēyākum.
akkālattē avarkaḷ, varalāṟu kūṟum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mikac ceytilar oṉṟiraṇṭu
ceytārāyiṉum avi tamiḻ vaḷaṅ keḻumiyavāyt tamiḻar maṉattik kavvuvaṉavāy illi. avarkaḷ
ulaka vāḻkkiyil veṟuppum, tuṟavu neṟiyil viruppum mikku iruntamiyiṉ tamiḻar
vāḻkkiyil nilaviya akapporuḷ nalaṅkaḷum, puṟapporuḷ tuṟikaḷum neṭunāḷkāṟum avarkaḷ
maṉattil iṭampeṟṟu, avarkaḷ aṟivi ēval koḷḷavilli. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ
ilakkaṇat tuṟiyiṟ pukuntu, ataṉ pakutikaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ceyyuḷiyalil peruntoṇṭu
purivārāyiṉar.
kaṟṟavar kallātavar eṉṟa irutiṟattārkkum aṟavuri vaḻaṅkumiṭattum uriyum pāṭṭumē mika
vēṇṭaṟpālaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉativiṭap piṉṉiyatē kallātavariyum eḷitiṟ piṇikkum
vāyppuṭiyatākum. ākavē, ataṟkuriya ceyyuḷiyalil perumpāṭupaṭṭup pala
putuneṟikaḷiyum iyalkaḷiyum kāṇpārāyiṉar. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyiṟ
kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa ceyyuḷiyal nūlkaḷuṭ pala, camaṇa muṉivarāṟ ceyyap peṟṟiruppataṟku
ituvē kāraṇamākum. ici nūlum nāṭaka nūlum kāmam payakkum ciṟumiyuṭiyavi eṉpatu
ammuṉivarkaḷiṉ karuttu, ataṉāl attuṟiyi nekiḻppatōṭu avvici nāṭakaṅkaḷil
paṟṟuṇṭākāmiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar. tamiḻar tāmum, tam tamiḻiyē maṟantu, vaṭanūṟ
katikaḷilum, vaṭavar vaḻakka voḻukkaṅkaḷilum vēṭki mītūrntu niṉṟamiyiṉ, avi
pēṇaṟpāṭiṉṟi iṟantaṉa.
tolkāppiya nūluṭ kāṇappaṭāta pāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ ceyta ceyyuḷiyalkaḷiṟ
kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu paḻiya tamiḻ neṟikaḷip putuneṟiyiṟ koṇartaṟku muyaṉṟu
peruntoṇṭu purinta ip periyōrkaḷ ici, nāṭakaṅkaḷilum tam karuttic
celuttiyiruppiṉ nam tamiḻ moḻiyil ettuṇiyō ici nūlkaḷum nāṭaka nūlkaḷum
tōṉṟiyirukkumē ici nūlkaḷuṭ cila ivarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
āṉāl, ataṉi uṇmiyeṉat tuṇitaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittil.
toṭakkattil tam camaya neṟikaḷi makkaṭku urippatē poruḷarkkik koṇṭu tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum
tirintu camaṇ paḷḷikaḷiyum, camaṇ pāḻikaḷiyum ivarkaḷ niṟuvi vantaṉar. avi yāvum
aṟaniliyaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉavēyaṉṟi, aṟivuc cuṭar vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ nūl niliyaṅkaḷāka
illāmiyāl, tēvārap periyōrkaḷ kālattil, ivaratu toṇṭu ivarkaṭkup pōtiya araṇ
ceyyātāyiṟṟu. camayappōr nikaḻccikaḷil, ivarkaṭkup pōtiya paṭiyākak kūṭiya tamiḻ
nūlkaḷ illi. ataṉāl ivarkaḷ eḷitil vellap peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷāka, ivarkaḷ ceyta
avinayam, intirakāḷiyam, parimāṇam mutaliyavi camaya nūlkaḷākavō &B aṟanūlkaḷākavō
payaṉpaṭavilli.
tēvāra kālattil ic camaṇa muṉivarkaḷuk kuṇṭākiya tōlvi ivarkaṭkup piṟkālattil
aḻiyāp pukaḻip payappatāyiṟṟu. ivarkaṭkut tam toṇṭil irunta kuṟipāṭukaḷ naṉku
pulaṉāyiṉa. tamiḻar kūṭṭattil nilaviya camaya nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu, avaṟṟippōla ilakkiyaṅ
kaḷiyum, ciṟu nūlkaḷiyum ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. iḷañ ciṟār mutal periyōr īṟākap
palarkkum payaṉpaṭattakka tamiḻ nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟat talippaṭṭaṉar. tamiḻt toṇṭiṉit tam camayat
toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum karuttiṉarākaliṉ, iḷiñarkkeṉa iyaṟṟiya ciṉṉūlkaḷilun
tam camayak karuttukaḷip pukuttu miṭamaṟintu pukuttit tam karuttu muṟṟa niṉintaṉar.
ciṟuvar kaṟkum kaṇakkiyal vāypāṭukaḷil, tam camayak karutti nuḻittaṟku iṭamillāmiyāl,
avaṟṟiṟkuk kāppuc ceyyuḷ yāttu, ataṉkaṇ camayavuṇarvip pukaṭṭiṉar.
"vaṉmatimukkuṭiyāṉmalaraṭitoḻa, nellaṇi lakkam neñciṉil varumē" eṉpatu nellilakkavāy
pāṭu. "arukaṉi amalaṉi acalaṉiyaṭitoḻa, ciṟukuḻi muppatum cintiyil varumē” eṉpatu
ciṟukuḻi vāypāṭu. ivvāṟu kalvi payiṉṟu varum ciṟuvarkaṭkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷik
kaṟpittaṟkut tōṟṟuvāyāka nikaṇṭukaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivar kālattukku muṉpiruntavi
nūṟpā vaṭivil perunūlkaḷāka iruntaṉa. avi, iciyoṭu viravi, putuppāviṉattiṟ
ceyyappaṭiṉ kaṟpārkku ūkkam mikum eṉṟu karutiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu
mutaliya nūlkaḷic ceytaṉar. itaṉkaṇṇum tam camayak karuttukkaḷi vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ
kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāppuc ceyyuḷ, "muṭivil iṉpattu mūvā mutalvaṉip pōṟṟi"c
ceytatu ulakaṟintatu. pakavaṉ eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ kūṟumiṭattu, "pakavaṉē īcaṉmāyōṉ
paṅkayaṉ ciṉaṉē puttaṉ” eṉat tokutturittaṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu tokutik kaṇ, "colvaki
eḻutteṇṇellām tollināḷ elliyāka, nalvakiyākkum piṇṭi nāṉmukaṉ nāḷum tīmi,
velviṉi toṭaṅkacceytu vīṭaruḷvōṉ tāḷ pōṟṟi” ceykiṉṟār. piṉṉar, nāṉilaṅkaḷ,
nālvakiyaṅkaṅkaḷi virittuk kūṟaluṟuvār, iṭiyē, camaṇ camayak karuttākiya nāṟkati
vakiyiṉi nuḻittu, "nāṟkati umpar makkaḷ colliya vilaṅkiṉōṭu narakar" eṉak kūṟiṉar.
ivvāṟē civarkaḷ kūṟum, civaṉukkuriya eṇvakik kuṇaṅkaḷik kūṟumiṭattu,
arukaṉukkuriya eṇvakik kuṇamum, iṟivaṉukku ākāveṉat tam camaya nūlkaḷ vilakkiya
eṇvakik kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya tolkāppiyam mika virintu paranta nūlāyk kiṭantamiyiṉ, ataṉic
ciṟuvarkaḷ eḷitil kaṟṟaṟkuriya niliyil ciṉṉūlum naṉṉūlum iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
ciṉṉūl ceyta kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar eṉṉum periyār vaṭanūṉ marapukaḷi oru ciṟitē taḻuvi, tam
camayap poruṭkuriya vaṇakkaṅ kūṟic cellukiṉṟār. naṉṉūl ceyta pavaṇanti muṉivar
curuṅkiya cilavāya coṟkaḷāl mika aḻakiyatāka itaṉic ceytuḷḷār. itaṉkaṇ, ivartam camayak
karuttik kāppuc ceyyuḷiṟ kāṭṭiyatōṭu nillātu, eḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇattilum
pukuttiyirukkiṉṟār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār “eḻutteṉappaṭupa akaramutal ṉakara
viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa" eṉat toṭaṅkiṉarēyaṉṟi, eḻuttāvatu iḵtu eṉa ilakkaṇaṅ
kūṟiṟṟilar. avvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappukunta pavaṇantiyār, "moḻimutaṟ kāraṇamām aṇuttiraḷ
oli eḻuttu" eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ "aṇuttiraḷ oli" eṉṟatu camaṇ camayak karuttu.
itaṉiyē eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappuk kūṟumiṭattum, ‘'niṟiyuyir muyaṟciyiṉ uḷvaḷi
turappa eḻum aṇuttiraḷ" eṉac ciṟuvar maṉattil naṉkupatiyak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
ittuṟik kaṇ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, “akatteḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭi, aḷapiṟkōṭal
antaṇar maṟittē; aḵtivaṇtuvalātu eḻuntu puṟatticikkum, meyteri vaḷiyici aḷavu
nuvaṉṟi.ciṉē" eṉṟē kūṟiṉar. tolkāppiyar kūṟiyataṉiyē avinayaṉārum, kuṇa
vīrapaṇṭitarum, puttamittiraṉārum kūṟiṉar. itaṉālum piṟkālac camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ
karuttut tam tamiḻt toṇṭu camayat toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivākum.
itaṉiyaṟintē ilakkaṇa viḷakkam iyaṟṟiya vittiyanāta tēcikar, "moḻik kāraṇamām
nātakāriyavoli eḻuttu" eṉak kūṟiṉārātal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, ivarkaḷ ceytaruḷiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ciṟṟilakkiyam, pērilakkiyam eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷ palavum antāti, kalampakam mutalākap palatiṟappaṭum. avi yāvum
camayap poruḷkaḷiyum, camaṇ camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷiyum pukaḻntu pāṭum ciṉṉūlkaḷākum.
tirukkalampakam, tirunūṟṟantāti mutaliyavi camaṇ camayattiṉar peritum pōṟṟip paṭikkum
tīviya centamiḻ nūlkaḷ. nariviruttam pōlum katic ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷum, mērumantara
purāṇam, yacōtara kāviyam pōlvaṉavum camaṇ camayap poruḷkaḷiyum varalāṟukaḷiyum viḷakkum
naṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷ.
cintāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷum, cūḷāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum
ciṟukāppiyaṅkaḷum tamiḻ moḻikkup potuvāka vittuk kūṟappaṭuvaṉa. ciṟu
kāppiyamintaṉuḷ, mēlē kāṭṭiya yacōtara kāviyamum oṉṟeṉak kūṟappaṭum.
ciṟukāppiyamintum tamiḻmoḻikkuriya potuk kāppiyaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭum centamiḻ nalam
ciṟantaviyāyiṉum, ivi yāvum camaṇ camayak karuttukkaḷiyē muṟṟum kūṟic celkiṉṟaṉa.
ivaṟṟuḷ cūḷāmaṇi eṉpatu taliyāyatu. itaṉi iyaṟṟiya periyār tōlāmoḻittēvar. ivar
peyarum, cīvaka cintāmaṇi ceyta tiruttakkatēvar peyarum iyaṟpeyaralla eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ.
ivarkaḷippōlat tamiḻt toṇṭu purinta camaṇa muṉivar peyarellām vaṭa moḻip peyarākavē
irukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar, amutacākarar, maṇṭalapuruṭar, vāmatēvar eṉpaṉa palavum
vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷē. ākavē, ivarkaḷ tamiḻ nūl payiṉṟu, tamiḻ vēṭki mikuntu,
tamiḻarpōlat tāmum tamiḻarākavē iyintu niṟkum tamiḻ nala mikutiyāl, tammit tamiḻp
peyarāl tiruttakkatēvar, tōlāmoḻittēvar. eṉpavarākak kūṟiṉārātal vēṇṭum. ivarkaṭkup
palavāṇṭukaṭkup piṟpōnta kāṉsṭāṉṭiṉas pescci eṉpārum, tamiḻt toṇṭupuriyum
cālpu eytiya vaḻit tammi vīramāmuṉivar eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭatu, eṉ karuttiṟku ātaravu
tarukiṉṟatu. iv vīramāmuṉivarum tēmpāvaṇi eṉṉum kāviyamum, toṉṉūl eṉṉum
ilakkaṇamum piṟavum ceytu tamiḻt toṇṭu purintirukkiṉṟār. cīvakacintāmaṇi yeṉpatu
peruṅkāppiya mintaṉuḷ taliyāyatu. ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ cilappatikāratti iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum
civarum, maṇimēkaliyic cīttalic cāttaṉār eṉṉum tamiḻarum eḻutiṉar. ēṉi iraṇṭum
iṟantuviṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ maṇimēkali putta camayak karuttukkaḷit taṉpāṟ koṇṭatu.
cilappatikārattum camaṇ camayak karuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. āyiṉum camayat toṇṭiṟkum tamiḻt
toṇṭiṟkum otta ilakkiyamāy viḷaṅkuvatu cīvakacintāmaṇiyē yākum.
cūḷāmaṇi, camaṇ camayattavrum ēṉic camayattavarum oppappōṟṟum takutiyuṭiyatāy
irukkiṟatu. itaṉi eḻutiya āciriyar, tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya puṟaccamayap perruḷkaḷiyum
camayap poruḷiyum kaṟṟu, tam camaya nūlkḷilum, puṟaccamaya nūlkaḷilum ottavāy uḷḷa katip
pakutikaḷik kaṇṭu, avaṟṟit tamiḻ makkaṭku uṇartti avar tam ātaravu peṟuvati nāṭiya
nallaṟiñarāvar. itaṉāl, ivar eḻutiya innūl iṉṟukāṟum naṉku pōṟṟap peṟṟu varukiṟatu.
cīvakacintāmaṇiyum ippeṟṟittāyc civar oruvarāl urikāṇavum peṟṟirukkiṟatu. .
tōlāmoḻit tēvar eḻutiya cūḷāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ, tiviṭṭaṉ, vicayaṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭu araca
kumārarkaḷ varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ varalāṟu vitika camayanūṟ katikaḷuḷ varum
varalāṟṟip peritum ottirukkiṟatu. iṭiyiṭiyē camaṇ camayak karuttu viravi niṟkiṟatu.
eṉiṉum, cintāmaṇi pōla, vitika camayaneṟi niṟpavarum virumpattakka attuṇi uvamanalam
uṭiyataṉṟu eṉṟē kūṟa vēṇṭum.
kaṇṇaṉ kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum, palarāmaṉ vaḷi vaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟum. ‘pālniṟa
uruviṟ paṉik koṭiyōṉum, nīlniṟa uruviṉ nēmiyōṉum eṉṟiru perunteyvamum
uṭaṉiṉṟāṅku (puṟam 5⁰8) eṉa varum tiviṭṭaṉik kaṇṭa makaḷir, "kaṭaloḷi maṇivaṇaṉ
kaṉaviṉil vantematu, uṭalakam veṟuvitāy uḷḷam vavviṉāṉ" (kumāra ⁰2) eṉṟum, vicayaṉik
kaṇṭavar "vārvaḷi vaṇṇaṉ em maṉattuḷāṉ piṟar, ērvaḷar neṭuṅ kaṇukku ilakkamallaṉāl" (⁰6)
eṉṟum kūṟiṉār eṉpar. itaṉāl niṟamottal kūṟiyavāṟām.
kaṇṇaṉ kañcaṉ viṭutta kuvalayā pīṭatti aṭarttāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ accuvakaṇṭaṉ viṭutta ciṅkatti
vāypiḷantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ peruki vanta maḻiyit taṭuppatu kuṟittuk kuṉṟiṉi yeṭuttuk
kuṭiyākap piṭittāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉum pulavar otiya varalāṟṟiṟkēṟpak koṭimācili eṉṉum
kuṉṟatti yeṭuttup pukaḻ niṟuviṉāṉ.
“erimaṇi kaṭakakki iraṇṭu mūṉṟiyap
perumaṇi nilampila mākak kīḻnuḻit
tarumaṇi neṭuvari yataṉi ēntiṉāṉ
tirumaṇi neṭumuṭic celvaṉ eṉpavē”
(aracu ⁰⁰⁰8⁰2) eṉac cūḷāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu.
kaṇṇaṉ piṉṉip pirāṭṭiyi maṇantāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ cuyampirapi eṉṉum vittiyātara makaḷi
maṇantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ cicupālaṉi āḻi yeṟintu veṉṟāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ ayakkirīvaṉi āḻi yeṟintu
veṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu tiviṭṭaṉ ceyal palavum kaṇṇaṉ ceyalkaḷōṭu ottiruttali,
“kōṭicili yeṭuttāṉ kōḷimāvāy pōḻntāṉ
āṭiyal yāṉi ayakkirīvaṉi aṭittāṉ
vīṭiṉ maṇiyaruvi veṇmaliyum
kippiṭittāṉ vāṭalil pūṅkaṇṇi māmēka vaṇṇaṉē.”
“valampurivāy vittāṉ vārcilikik koṇṭāṉ,
calampuri caṇṭi talipaṇippuk kaṇṭāṉ,
polampuri tāmariyāḷ poṉṉākam tōyntāṉ,
kalampurivaṇṭaṭakkik kārmēka vaṇṇaṉē" (cuyam ⁰⁰⁰⁰8)
eṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu kāṇalām.
iṉi, ipperiyōrkaḷuḷ tiruttakkatēvar ēṉiyōrik kāṭṭilum ulakiyalil mikka tērcci
yuṭiyavarāka viḷaṅkiyirukkiṟār. tamiḻnāṭṭil tām parappak karutum camayap poruḷ
kuṟitta inta nūl, tamiḻnāṭṭil paraviyirukkum camayap poruḷip
puṟaṅkūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum, tam camayap poruḷiyē niralpaṭak kūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum,
tamiḻarkaḷāl ātarikkappaṭuvatu illiyām eṉpati naṉku uṇarn tiruntaṉar. am
matinuṭpattāl, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ payiṉṟirunta vitika camayak kati nikaḻccikaḷi innūṟkaṇ
uvamamukattāṟ payila vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl innūl camayak koḷkiyāl vēṟupaṭṭa tamiḻar
palarālum ātarikkappeṟṟa teṉpatu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum katikaḷāl nām aṟikiṉṟōm.
ivviyalpiṉik kaṭippiṭiyāmiyāṟṟāṉ, tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam mutaliyaṉa tamiḻariṭiyē
naṉku vaḻaṅkātiruk kiṉṟaṉa pōlum. ivviyalpiṉi mūṉṟu iyalkaḷāṟ kāṭṭi ikkaṭṭuriyi
muṭikkiṉṟōm.
tēvar kālattē, tamiḻar tēvarkaḷ imiyā nāṭṭam uṭiyareṉṟum, avarkaṭkum acurarkaṭkum
pakimiyiruntu vantateṉṟum, ataṉāl tēvarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi tuṉpuṟṟaṉa reṉṟum, tēvarkaḷ
tirumāliṉ utaviyāl kaṭal kaṭintu iṉiya amutam peṟṟu makiḻntaṉareṉṟum kēṭṭiruntaṉar.
an nikaḻccikaḷit tiruttakkatēvar tām camayat toṇṭu kuṟittup -
cīvi.ka.cintāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ caccantaṉik kollak karuti
amiccarōṭu cūḻnta kālattil amiccaruḷ tarumatattaṉ eṉpāṉ kūṟṟil vittu, "iṟaṅku
kaṇṇimiyār viḻittēyiruntu, aṟṅkaḷ vavva ataṉpuṟam kākkalār" (2⁰⁰4⁰8) eṉṟum,
tēvarkaḷ kaṭal kaṭintu amutu peṟṟa ceytiyi, patumukaṉ kōvintiyi maṇantu kūṭik
kūṟum nayappuriyil, "tīmpāṟ kaṭalit tiripoṅkak kaṭintu tēvar, tāmpāṟ paṭutta
amutō" eṉṟum, akkaṭal nākam kayiṟākak koṇṭu kaṭiyappaṭṭateṉpati cīvakaṉ kāntaruvatattiyi
maṇanta kāliyil, kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉatu ēvalāl, accīvakaṉi etirtta vēntar avaṉāl alippuṇṭa
ceytiyi viḷakkutaṟku uvamiyāka niṟutti, “aruvari nākañcuṟṟi āḻiyāṉ kaṭiyavaṉṟu,
karuvari kuṭiyappaṭṭa kaṭaleṉak kalaṅki vēntar... uṭiyaluṟṟār" (8⁰⁰⁰2) eṉṟu
kūṟukiṉṟār.
iraṇṭāvatāka, murukaṉ tāmarippūvil tōṉṟiyavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ malināṭṭukkuriya kaṭavuḷ
eṉṟum, avaṉ ēntiya vēl vali ciṟantatu eṉṟum, avaṉ āṭavariṟ pērāṟṟalilum pēraḻakilum
mēmpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻar camayavulaku kūṟip pārāṭṭi vantatu. ic ceyalkaḷit tiruttakka
tēvar, “pūviṉuṭ piṟanta tōṉṟal puṇṇiyaṉ” (3⁰⁰⁰6) eṉṟum, “tāmari amarar mēvarat
tōṉṟiya aṇṇal” (6⁰⁰9⁰4) eṉṟum, "maṇaṅkoḷ pūmici mivari mintaṉō" (⁰⁰⁰6⁰⁰⁰1) eṉṟum,
"amaruḷ āṉātu, okkiya murukaṉ eḵkam ōriraṇṭaṉiya kaṇṇāḷ" (⁰⁰⁰2⁰⁰6⁰1) eṉṟum, “cempoṟ
kaṭampaṉ cevvēlum” (⁰⁰⁰679⁰4) eṉṟum, "katampaṉē murukaṉ vēṟpōr" (⁰⁰⁰6⁰⁰8⁰1) eṉṟum,
"tiruvari mārpaṉ tiṇtēr maññiyē murukaṉtāṉ" (8⁰⁰⁰2) eṉṟum, “vaḷḷal māt taṭintāṉ
aṉṉa māṇpiṉāṉ" (⁰⁰⁰2⁰6) eṉṟum, "muntu cūrtaṭinta murukaṉ nampi eṉpār" (2⁰⁰⁰5⁰⁰4⁰8)
eṉṟum, ivi pōlap piṟavāṟum kūṟic ciṟappittirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟi iṭaṅkūṟi
viḷakkaluṟiṉ kaṭṭuri viriyum.
iṉi, civaperumāṉik kūṟumiṭattup "pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiyaṉ" (3⁰⁰62) eṉṟum, "kāriyuṇṭik
kaṭavuḷ" (6⁰⁰7⁰) eṉṟum, “aṟavāḻi yaṇṇal” eṉṟum, piṟavum kūṟiyum pārāṭṭuvar. paramaṉ
kaṇṇutalāl kāmaṉik kāynta ceyti "kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷ cīṟak kaṉalerippaṭṭa kāmaṉ" (⁰⁰⁰3⁰⁰⁰1)
eṉpataṉālum, purameritta varalāṟṟiṉi, “kaṭimatil mūṉṟumeyta kaṭavuḷ" (⁰⁰⁰87)
eṉpataṉālum, "kaṇiyeri yaḻalam peyta kaṇṇutal mūrtti yottāṉ" (2⁰⁰⁰2⁰⁰46) eṉpataṉālum,
maṅki paṅkiṉaṉāy niṟkum niliyiṉi, "piṟiyaṇi yaṇṇal koṇṭapeṇṇorpāl koṇṭa tottār"
(utaṭuḷakaḷ) eṉpataṉālum piṟavāṟṟālum pukaḻntuḷḷār.
iṉi, tirumāl, paramaṉ purameritta kālattil ampāyiṉa ceytiyi, “pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiya
ṉeyta kaṇiyēpōl, mākamīṉṟa māmatiyaṉṉāṉ vaḷarkiṉṟāṉ" (kācu) eṉṟam, avaṟkuk karuṭappuḷ
ūrtiyeṉpati, "āḻiyāṉ ūrtippuḷ" (cācacu) eṉṟum, avaṉ cakkarappaṭi yuṭiyaṉ eṉpataṉi
itaṉālum peṟavittum, tirumāl kaṇṇaṉāyt tōṉṟiya kālattu, pāratap pōril caṅkōci
eḻuppiya ceytiyi, "ceṅkaṇmāl teḻikkappaṭṭa valampurit turuvaṅ koṇṭa, caṅku" (aāmaka)
eṉṟum, kaṇṇaṉik kōṟaṟkup pōnta kuvalayāpīṭameṉṟa yāṉiyi avaṉ aṭartta nikaḻcciyiṉi,
"mallal nīrmaṇi vaṇṇaṉip paṇṭornāḷ, kolla vōṭiya kuñcaram pōṉṟatac, celvaṉ pōṉṟaṉaṉ
cīvakaṉ"(kūāaci) eṉṟum, tirumāl irāmaṉākiya kālattu, avaṉ marāmaramēḻum eyta vil
vaṉmiyi, "marāmara mēḻum eyta vāṅkuvil taṭakki valvil, irāmaṉi vallaṉ eṉpatu iciyalāṟ
kaṇṭatilli" (tacuācaki) eṉa uvamittum makiḻkiṉṟār.
mēlum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya civaperumāṉum, tirumālum, tām paravum arukamūrttiyē
eṉavum, avviruvariyum paravuvōr, arukaṉip paravutal vēṇṭum eṉpār,
"malarēntu cēvaṭiya māleṉpa mālāl
alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭuvār alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭumēl ilarē malareṉiṉum
ēttāvā ṟeṉṉē
"kaḷicēr kaṇiyuṭiya kāmaṉiyum kāynta aḷicēr aṟavāḻi aṇṇal ivaṉeṉpa, aḷicēr aṟavāḻi
aṇṇal ivaṉēl, viḷiyāk kuṇattuti nām vittālāṟeṉṉē" (tucuṉaci.ke) eṉṟu
moḻintirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, ivar kāṭṭi makiḻvikkum tamiḻ nūṟ collāṭcikaḷum, tamiḻar vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅkaḷum
īṇṭu eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ, iḵtōr perum parappiṉatākum.
-
ivvāṟu, tam camayap poruḷi muṟṟa viritturikkum peruṅkāppiyam pāṭat ataṉit tamiḻ
nāṭṭavar peritum virumpiyēṟṟup pēṇattakka viraku aṟintu amitta amiti, avaratu
tamiḻ māṇpulamiyiṉi naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. mēlum, ivar kāṭṭiya neṟiyiṉi ivarkkup
piṉ vantōr kaṭippiṭittirupparēl, camayap pūcalkaḷum, piṟa tīmikaḷum uḷavātaṟ
kētuvilliyākiyirukkum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, camaṇamuṉivar ciṉa camayattuc cāṉṟōr eṉpatum, avarkaḷ nam
tamiḻnāṭṭil tam camaya neṟiyi uṇarttap pukuntapōtu, tamiḻar, ipperiyārkaṭkup pala
āṇṭukaḷ muṉpē vanta vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl tam tamiḻccamaya neṟi maṟantu, vaṭavar camayak
koḷkiyilum, vaṭanūṉ marapukaḷilum vēṭkiyuṟṟut tam tamiḻ vaḷarcciyik karutātu
niṉṟaṉar eṉpatum, āyiṉum camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ tēvāra kālam variyil tamiḻt toṇṭu
puritaṟkaṇ ūkkaṅ koḷḷātiruntaṉar eṉpatum, piṉpu akkālattu vātaṅkaḷil tōlviyuṟṟataṉ
payaṉāl, tamiḻt toṇṭu puriyatu toṭaṅki, ciṉṉūlkaḷum perunūlkaḷum ceytaṉar eṉpatum,
ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ cila putuneṟik karuttukkaḷip pukuttiyum, tām ceyta nūlkaḷuḷ
vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil tam camayak karuttukkaḷ nuḻittum camayat toṇṭu virittaṉar eṉpatum,
atuvē nam tamiḻukkup perun toṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatu piṟavumākum. inneṟiyik kippaṟṟiya
piṉ vanta kiṟistavap periyārkaḷum tamiḻt toṇṭu purintaṉar. ikkālattu ēṉic
camayattavarkaḷum immuṟiyi mēṟkoḷvārkaḷāyiṉ, taṅkaḷ camayaneṟi vaḷam peṟuvatōṭu nam tamiḻ
moḻiyum ciṟappeytum eṉpatu uṟuti.
---------------

19. intiya nāṭṭil iculām ceyta iṉiya toṇṭu

nām vāḻum ivviṉiyanāṭu intiya nāṭu eṉpatil namakku iṉpam eḻukiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ
vāḻum nām aṉivarum intiyar eṉṟu eṇṇumpōtu, nam uḷḷattil iṉpak kiḷarcci
eḻumpiyarumpukiṟatu. namatu camayam, vāḻkki muṟi, oḻukkam eṉpaṉavaṟṟi niṉintu
eḻutumpōtu nammiyaṟiyāta uvaki kaṉintu ūṟukiṟatu. ittuṇic ciṟappukku mutaliṭamāy,
nām iruntu vāḻtaṟkut tāyakamāy viḷaṅkum nam intiya nāṭu oruvakiyil varuttattiyum
nalkukiṉṟatu, nam kūṭṭattil camayak kāyccal, vakuppup pūcal, uyarvu tāḻvu, tīṇṭutal
tīṇṭāmi mutaliya tīkkōḷkaḷ niṉṟu nām peṟum vāḻkki yiṉpattic citikkiṉṟaṉa. piṟa
camayaṅkaḷum, piṟacamayattavarkaḷum ceytuḷḷa naṉmikaḷik kāytal, uvattal iṉṟi
naṭuniliyilniṉṟu kāṇa voṭṭātu, ivikaḷ maṟikkiṉṟaṉa; maṟittaṉavar eṉpatip paṟṟi iṉi
niṉippatu payaṉil ceykiyākum, maṟikkātirukkumāṟu ceytalē ceyyaṟpālatākum.
nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum kāṇpatē nam aṟiviṉ kaṭamiyākum. uyariya poṉ mikattāḻnta
maṇṇoṭu kalantu kāṇapapaṭukiṟataṉṟō? mikavuyarnta pālum orō vaḻit tīmipayakkak
kāṇkiṉṟōmallamō? naṉporuḷ iḻintōriṭattum, iḻiporuḷ uyarntōriṭattum peṟukiṉṟōm;
keṭuporuḷ naṇpar vāyilum, naṉ poruḷ pakivarpālum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉavē! ivaṟṟiyuṭkoṇṭu,
nam tiruvaḷḷuvap peruntaki, "epporuḷ yār yār vāykkēṭpiṉum apporuḷ meypporuḷ
kāṇpatu aṟivu" eṉa moḻintatu poyyā moḻiyaṉṟō! ivvuṇmiyik kaṟpatum, piṟarkkuk
kaṟpippatum ceyyum nām, piṟa camayattavar ceyta nalaṅkaḷiyaṟintu pārāṭṭuvatu namakkuk
kaṭamiyākum. nammilum, ‘'cīṟāppurāṇam" eṉṉum tīviya centamiḻk kāviyamum,
"nākūrppurāṇam" eṉṉum nalañ ceṟi purāṇamum peṟṟa tamiḻaṟiñar, iculām camayattavar nam
tamiḻkkāṟṟiya perun toṇṭiṉi yaṟintu makiḻum peruntakikaḷātaliṉ, avarkaḷ,
iccamayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭiṟkē ceytuḷḷa perum paṇikaḷit terintu pōṟṟum
ciṟappuṭiyavar eṉak karutukiṉṟēṉ.
iculām camayattavar mucalmāṉkaḷ eṉavum, makamatiyar eṉavum nam nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkappeṟuvar.
avarkaḷ mēṟ koṇṭirukkum iculām camayamum mukamatiyamatam eṉṟum kūṟuppaṭum. iccamayam,
puttacamayam, camaṇacamayampōla, intu camayattiṉiṉṟu muḷitta kiḷic camayamaṉṟu; itu, ciṟitu
kālattukku muṉpu nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu paravirukkum kiṟittava camayattukkum muṉṉar
nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu ciṟappeytiya camayamākum. nam nāṭṭuc camaya nūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭum
aṟuvakippaṭṭa akaccamayam, puṟaccamayam, akappuṟac camayam, puṟappuṟac camayam eṉṟa camayat
tokivaki virikaḷil, iḵtu iṭampeṟāmikkum ituvē kāraṇamākum. itaṟkum kiṟittava
camayattukkum mutaṟcamayam yūtacamayam eṉṟu kūṟupa. avyūtacamayam, intiyanāṭṭiṟ piṟantu
taḻitta camayamaṉṟu; ataṉāl, ataṉippaṟṟi nam nāṭṭuc camayak kṇakkarkaḷ ārāycci ceytilar.
piṟkālattup pōnta camayap pulavarkaḷum avvarāycciyiṉic ceyyātoḻintaṉar,
ceytiruppārāyiṉ, nam camūkattil pirippiṉṟi vāḻum ciṉar, puttar
mutaliyavarkaḷippōla, iccamayattavarkaḷum nammoṭu pirippaṟak kalantukoṇṭiruppar. āṉāl,
itaṟku vēṟu kāraṇam kūṟupavarum uṇṭu. āyiṉum aḵtu attuṇic ciṟappāka illi.
ataṉiyum piṉṉark kūṟuvōm.
iculām eṉpatu oruteyvak kōṭpāṭṭik kaṭip piṭiyākavuṭiya peruñ camayamākum.
puṟaccamayaṅkaḷiyum palateyvak kōṭpāṭukaḷiyum evvakiyilum ēlātatu; param poruḷ oṉṟit
tavirap piṟa evaṟṟiyum vaṇaṅkutaṟku makkaḷip paṇiyāta paṇpu mēmpaṭṭatu; kiṟittava camayamum
inniliyil itaṉōṭu otta iyalpuṭiyatu. iviyiraṇṭaṟkum tāyccamayamāka irunta
yūtacamayampōla, iviyuṇarttum iyavuḷ, ēṉit teyvaṅkaḷ uḷaveṉum eṇṇutaṟkum iṭantarāta
uyarkaṭavuḷ eṉṉiṉ atu mikiyākātu. "pallōrum paramporuḷ oṉṟē uṇṭu; ataṉpāl
parivukoṭu paracivāḻtal ellōrum cey kaṭaṉām” eṉṟē meymmineṟiyi ulakil
vaṟpuṟuttiya peruneṟi itaṟkē uṇṭu. ettiṟattu makkaḷiyum uyarvu, tāḻvu iṉṟi oppa
eṇṇiyoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉṟa uyarneṟiyil oppaṟṟa camayam iculām camayamē. kaṭavuḷiṉ
aruṭkaṇṇiṉmuṉ makkaḷ vēṟṟumiyilli eṉṟa cenneṟiyi ulakil niṟuvi, yāvarum ataṉik
kaṭippiṭit toḻukac ceyta mutaṟperumi itaṟkē uṇṭu.
iccamayam, paṇṭik kālattil, kiṟittu piṟanta cumār aṟunūṟu yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, arapiya
nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta makamatu eṉṟa perumakaṉārāl mutaṉmutalākak kāṇappeṟṟatu. akkālattu,
avvarapiya nāṭṭumakkaḷiṭiyē poruntāc ceykikaḷum, tiruttāk koḷkikaḷum palavāy
malintu, uruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉ peyarāl, annāṭṭiṟkup pēriḻiviṉic ceytu vantaṉa. aruḷ
vaḷḷalākiya ipperiyār avaṟṟik kaṇṭu uḷampoṟātu iccamaya vuṇmiyiṉik kaṇṭu, am makkaḷi
nalvaḻiyil teruṭṭit tiruppaveṇṇiṉār. avar muyaṟci toṭakkattil avaratu
arumiyuyirkkē iṉṉali viḷivikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avarkaḷ viḷitta tīmiyiṉip poṟāta
avar, muṭivil, annāṭṭiṉ cīriya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya mekkā nakariviṭṭu nīṅki metiṉā
eṉṉum nakarukkuc ceṉṟār. ataṟkuḷ annāṭṭavaruṭpalar, avar teruṭṭiyacamayak koḷkiyi
mēṟkoṇṭu, avar kāṭṭiya neṟiyil oḻukattalippaṭṭaṉar. avarkaṭkumakamatiyar eṉṟum,
apputiya camayattiṟku makamatiya camayam eṉṟum peyarkaḷ muṟiyē vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. mekkāvi
nīṅki, avar, metiṉāvukkuc ceṉṟa nikaḻcci, iṉṉum. ijirā eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. avar
vaḻaṅkiya arumi moḻikaḷ, “kurāṉ” eṉṉum peyaruṭiya arumaṟiyil poṟikkap
paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. makamatiyarkaḷ, ammoḻikaḷ paramporuḷ, makamatu napiyiṉ vāyvaḻiyāka
vaḻaṅkiyavi eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.
intukkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nāṉmaṟikaḷi āṇṭavaṉē aruḷiṉāṉ eṉṟum, ataṉāl avaṉukku "vētaṅkaḷ
moḻintapirāṉ" eṉappeyarum kūṟupavarākaliṉ, avarkaṭku itaṉkaṇ putumiyoṉṟum tōṉṟātu.
"aruḷneṟi" yākiya accamayamum, aruḷ neṟiyi uṇarttum iyalpu paṟṟi "iculām" camayam
eṉṟē vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu. ic camayam vaḷara vaḷara, arapiyar iṭiyil irunta tīneṟikaḷum
tīyoḻukkaṅkaḷum nīṅkiṉa; avarkaḷ aṉivarum uṭaṉ piṟantāreṉavuṇarntu tammuḷ oṟṟumiyuṇṭu
paṇṇik koṇṭaṉar. taṅkaṭkup perunalam ceyta iccamayavuṇarvi nilavulakil yāṇṭum parappa
vēṇṭumeṉṟa vēṭkimikku, oru nūṟu yāṇṭukaḷukkuḷ, pāracīkam, turukkittāṉam,
āpkaṉittāṉam eṉṟa iṉṉōraṉṉa nāṭṭumakkaḷ aṉivariyum makamati camayattavarākkik koṇṭaṉar.
pāracīkarum, nam intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil akkālattu malkiyirunta āriyarkaḷip pōla,
tīyiṉik kaṭavuḷākak karuti vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkuṭiyarāyiruntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭ cilar,
immakamatiya camayattimēṟ koḷḷātavarāy, intiyanāṭṭuṭ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. makamatiyarum
neṭuṅkālam variyil, ip pāracīkaruṭaṉum, tārttaruṭaṉum, ēṉiya vaṭapulattavaruṭaṉum pōr
nikaḻttiya vaṇṇamēyiruntamiyiṉ, nam intiya nāṭṭil, ippoḻutikkuc cumār
toḷḷāyiramāṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ varamāṭṭātu niṉṟaṉar; piṉṉarē, avarkaḷ nam intiya nāṭṭiṟku
varalāyiṉar.
ivarkaḷ, innāṭṭiṟku varutaṟku muṉpē pāracīkaruṭ cilar pōntu kuṭiyēṟi viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu
muṉpu kūṟiyuḷḷōm. avarkaṭku muṉ vēṟupala nāṭṭiṉarum vantu iṅkē
kuṭipukuntiruntaṉar. avarkaṭkum, makamatiyarkaṭkum periyatoru vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. muṉṉē
vantavarkaḷ tam camaya vuṇarvi nāṭṭiṟ parappavēṇṭumeṉṟa nōkkamuṭiya varallar; ivarkaṭkup
piṉṉarp pōnta airōppiyarum avvāṟu vantavarallar; ivarkaḷ maṭṭil camayat toṇṭiṉik
kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. mēlum ivarkaṭku
muṉṉē pōntavar, nam nāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntu vāḻntirunta ēṉi makkaḷōṭu kūṭi,
orumiyuṟṟu, avarkaṭkuriya oḻukkam, vaḻipāṭu mutaliya neṟikaḷit tāmum ēṟṟu,
intiyarōṭu intiyarāy iyintiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ yavaṉar, skitiyar, maṅkōliyar, pārttiyar
mutaliya palarāvar. avarkaḷ nāṭcellac cella, intukkaḷuḷ intukkaḷāy, tam peyar, moḻi,
camayam, oḻukkam, uṭi, maṉappāṉmi eṉṟa palavaṟṟiṉum oṉṟiviṭṭaṉar. kiṟittu piṟappataṟku
iru nūṟuyāṇṭukaṭku muṉ innāṭṭiṟkup pōnta kirēkka tūtar oruvar, innāṭṭiṟ
palaviṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟār. avar tanti 3 à N c N G2 (Dion) eṉṟum, avar peyar hīliyō
ṭōras (Helio dorus) eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar oru camayam vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa tirumāl
kōyil oṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avari vaḻipaṭṭu, ammaṭṭil nillātu, oru "kōpuramum"
eṭuppittār. piṉṉar, avar oru viṇava pākavatarāy, tirumāli vaḻipaṭṭu vantār. ivarip
piṉpaṟṟip pōntavar palarum, ivvaṇṇamē vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu vēṟṟunāṭṭu makkaṭkum
iyintatākum eṉṟu karutiṉar. icceyti pēs nakari (Bas nagar) luḷḷa tuṇiṟ cetukkap
peṟṟirukkum kalveṭṭiṉāl namakkut terikiṉṟatu.
makamatiya, camayattiṉar, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntapōtu, ikkaruttu nam nāṭṭila nilaviyirunta-
tāyiṉum, ivarkaḷ ataṉi mēṟkoḷḷavilli. iculām camayam kūṟum "allā" tirumāliṉ
palvēṟu piṟappukkaḷuḷ oṉṟām eṉṟum, ic camaya vuṇmikaḷi yulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyaruḷiya
makamatu perumāṉit tirumālaṭiyāruḷ oruvareṉṟum kūṟi, intukkaḷ ivarkaḷit tammuḷ
oruvarāka iyittuk koḷḷa muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyuḷ vallunar, “allōpa niṣat"
eṉṟoru nūli yeḻuti, makamatiya vēntaruṭ ciṟantavarāṉa akpar eṉṟa peyar koṇṭa aracar
perumāṉi, “avatāra mūrtti" eṉṟum collat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, makamatiyar, ivi, tam
camayak karuttukaṭku muraṇātal kaṇṭu, ivaṟṟi aṟavē ēlārāyiṉar. avarkaḷ, intukkaḷāṟ
ceyyappaṭum “caṭaṅku"kaḷiyum uṭaṉ paṭṭilar. “pala teyva vaḻipāṭu ceypavar tūyarallar;
tūyatallāta yātum kāpāvil pukuvatum tītu" eṉṟu kurāṉ moḻivati avar viṭātu
pōṟṟiṉar.
ivvāṟu, tiṇṇiya camayavuṇarviṉik koṇṭiruntataṉāl, ivarkaḷ, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu
kuṭiyēṟiya piṉpum, tamatu vaḻakkam, oḻukkam, peyar, moḻi eṉṟa palavakiyilum
intukkaḷiṟ pirintē yiruntu varalāyiṉar. orē nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu vāḻntu vanta
pōtum, avar innāṭṭavarōṭu kalappiṉṟiyē iruntaṉar. iv vēṟupāṭṭi nīkkik kalappiyuṇṭu
paṇṇum tuṟiyil emmuyaṟciyum payaṉpaṭātu pōyiṟṟu. intiya nāṭṭiṟpukuntu kuṭiyēṟi
vāḻntuvarum intiya makamatiyar aṉivarum, tam camayavuṇarvu nekiḻātu pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
ataṉāl, avar tam uḷḷam, avaratu camayam piṟanta nāṭṭiyē nāṭi niṟpatāyiṟṟu, nāṭōṟum,
avarkaḷ ceyyum kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum, toḻukiyilum avartam mukam arapiyanāṭṭu
mekkāviyē nōkki niṟkiṟatu; avartam maṉappāṉmi, oḻukkamuṟi, āṭcimuṟi, camayak kalvi
ākiyayāvum arēpiyā, ciriyā, pāracīkam, ekiptu eṉṟa innāṭṭu iyalpiṉavākavē
irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷuṭiya camaya moḻi, camaya cakāptam, ilakkiyam, āciriyaṉmār,
aṭikaṇmār, kōyilkaḷ eṉṟa yāvum orē vakiyākavē irukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivarkaḷ,
innilavulakil eppuṟattil vāḻiṉum, iviyellām oru peṟṟiyavāy iruppatu kāṇappaṭumē
tavira avvannāṭṭu ellikku akappaṭṭu, āṅkē aṭaṅki oṭuṅkiyiruttalik kāṇṭal aritu.
ivartam varukikkum, ivarkaṭku muṉpōnta ēṉiyōr varukikkum irunta vēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ
mēlē kāṭṭiyatu taliciṟantatākum. itaṉōṭu oppap piṟitoru vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. nam
nāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiyarkaḷ, āyirattu irunūṟu mutal āyirattu aṟunūṟām
āṇṭuvariyil, araciyal, camūkaviyal eṉṟa ellāvaṟṟilum, pōrttiṟamum nāṭōṭikaḷāy
niliyiṉṟit tiriyum tiṟamumē nirampak koṇṭiruntaṉar. pācaṟiyiṟ ṟaṅkum aracar
tāṉipōlavē, makamatiya araciyaṟ ṟalivarkaḷum āṅkāṅku iruntu vantaṉar. oru
niliyāyttaṅki, nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷic cīrceytu, araciyaliṉ nilimiyi niṟuvi,
nāṭukāttal, tīyari yoṟuttal ākiya ceyalkaḷōṭu camūkattuṟiyilum nalam pala ceyyat
toṭaṅkiyatu piṟkālattiltāṉ nikaḻvatāyiṟṟu. kalappu maṇam, camayak kāyccaliṉmi,
ottavurimi mutaliya pala naṉmikaḷum piṉṉarē uṇṭāyiṉa.
uyarntōrkaḷiṭattil orukuṟi uṇṭākumāyiṉ, atu virivil ulakaṟiyap parantuviṭum.
avvaṇṇamē, oruvaṉ ceyta kuṟṟam ulakaṟiyat tōṉṟi nilipeṟumāyiṉ, avaṉ pāl oruvakic
ciṟappuṇṭeṉpatu tuṇintukoḷḷalām. ammuṟiyē, makamatiya maṉṉarkaḷ kālattuc ciṟuceyalkaḷ
cila, intiya nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuc ciṟu nūlkaḷuṭ kāṇap paṭumāyiṉ, avaṟṟik koṇṭē,
avarpāl amintirunta perumi iṉitu uṇarappaṭukiṉṟatu. taṉ uyirik kollutaṟkup pōnta
aracaputtiraṉ oruvaṉip poṟuttu vīraṉākkic ciṟappitta āṇṭaki pāpar vēntari aṟiyātār
yāvar? intu camayattavariyum makamatiya camayattavariyum oppamatittu uyarnalam purinta aracar
peruntakiyāṉa akpari aṟiyātavar aṟivu peṟātavar allarō? apperuntakikaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷa
nalam palavum eḻutappukiṉ, ikkaṭṭuri varampiṉṟi virivatallatu avartam pukaḻnalam
aṭaṅkiviṭum eṉa eṇṇuvatu ciṟumiyaṉṟō? innalamaṉittiṟkum aṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷatu iculām
camayattiṉ iyalpē eṉpati maṟattal ākātu.
intiyanāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyākiya nam tamiḻnāṭṭi āṇṭa muṭiyuṭivēntar mūvaruḷ cōḻarkaḷ
kālattil, nilappaṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyum iruntu nāṭukāttuvanta ceyti nām aṉivarum
aṟintatākum. ‘kāntaḷūrccāli kalamaṟuttaruḷiya’ irācarācaṉ mutaliya cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ,
kaṭaṟpaṭi koṇṭu ceṉṟu pakippulaṅkaḷi veṉṟaṉar. avarkaṭkup piṉ vanta cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ
vīḻcciyaṭiyavē, kaṭaṟpaṭiyum keṭṭaḻintatu. piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭiya kūṭṭuṟavum avarkaḷ
kālattiṟkuppiṉ kiṭippatu aritākiyatu. āyiṉum ikkaṭaṟpaṭiyum, piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭiya
kūṭṭuṟavum im makamatiya maṉṉar perumakkaḷāl maṟuvalum uṇṭāyiṉa.
nam intiyanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ, putta camaya kālattil ēṉi nāṭukaḷiṟ paravi
avaṟṟōṭu cīriya toṭarpu peṟṟiruntaṉar. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil attoṭarpi
nekiḻttu, tam nāṭṭellikkuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiyamintu viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, innāṭu
taṉṉiliyil aṭaṅki, piṟavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpiṉṟit taṉippatāyiṟṟu. paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil,
iculām camayattavariṉ peruntuṇiyāl, attoṭarpiṉi mīṭṭum nam nāṭu peṟṟatu; āyiṉum
attoṭarpu paṇṭē yiruntatu pōṉṟilli. putta camaya, kālattil nam nāṭṭavar vēṟu
nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟatupōla, ikkālattil celvatu oḻintaṉar. pala āyirakkaṇakkiṉarāṉa vēṟṟu
nāṭṭumakkaḷ nam nāṭṭiṟku varattoṭaṅkiṉar. makamatiya camayattavar māttiram vēṟu
-----------
nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu varuvavati mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. ivar tam araciyal, patiṉeṭṭām
nūṟṟāṇṭil taḷarcciyaṭintu vīḻvuṟṟatu nām aṟivōmaṉṟō? atuvariyil, vaṭa mēṟkuk
kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyākap pokārā, cāmalkaṇṭu, pālku, kurācāṉ, kivāricum, pāracīkam
eṉṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu makkaṭ kūṭṭam nam nāṭṭiṟku vantu koṇṭiruntatu. annāḷil
āpkāṉittāṉamum ṭilli vēntariṉ āṭciyiltāṉ aṭaṅkiyiruntatu. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ
toṭakkattil, jahāṅkīr eṉṉum maṉṉaratu āṭcik kālattil, nam intiya nāṭṭiliruntu pōlaṉ
kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka āṇṭutōṟum 14000 oṭṭakac cumiyaḷaviṉavāya poruḷ, kantakār, pāracīkam
eṉṟa nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟaṉa. “mēlikkaṭaṟ kariyil irunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (Sea ports)
veḷināṭṭuk kaṭaṟ pōkku varavukku vāyil tiṟantiruntaṉa. kīḻikkaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷuḷ,
macūlippaṭṭiṉam āyirattu aṟunūṟṟēḻām āṇṭuvarik kōlakoṇṭāvi yāṇṭa cultāṉkaḷukku
uriyatāyiruntatu. ataṟkup piṉpē atu mukalāya vēntarkaṭku uriyatāyiṟṟu.
appaṭṭiṉattiliruntu, ilaṅki, cumittiri, cāvakam, cīyam, cīṉam eṉṟa nāṭukaṭku vāṇipam
pēṇi, vaṅkaṅkaḷ pala ceṉṟa vaṇṇamiruntaṉa eṉa mukalāyarāṭci (Moghul administration)
eṉṟa nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭa nāṭṭil mukalāya muṭimaṉṉarkaḷiṉ āṭci nilaviya kālam irunūṟuyāṇṭukaḷēyākum;
āyiṉum, akkālattil nam nāṭṭiṟku uṇṭākiya nalaṅkaḷ palavākum. pala vēṟu vaki moḻi
vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṟku araciyal moḻi yoṉṟu potuvāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. aḵtu annāṭṭu
moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyiṉ, piṟamoḻi pēcuvōr kūṭṭattu amiti uṇṭākātu eṉavuṇarntu,
immaṉṉarkaḷ tamatu pāracīka moḻiyiṉiyē araciyal moḻiyāka vakuttaṉar. piṉṉar atu palarkkum
tāymoḻiyām nilimiyiṉiyum aṭivatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl āṭcimuṟiyum nāṇayac celāvaṇiyum
oru muṟiyāy naṭipeṟalāyiṉa. iṉṟum, kaccēri, jillā, tālukā, pirkkā, tāciltār,
ucūr eṉa vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ palavum avartam āṭcineṟiyiṉ orumi muṟiyiṉi viḷakkum
aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷākum. nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭi rekṭā eṉappaṭum intuttāṉimoḻi vaḻaṅki vantatu.
ivarkaḷ kālattil, vintavarikku vaṭakkiluḷḷanāṭu, pala ciṟṟaracark kurimiyuṟṟu, pala
ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirintu, eppoḻutum pōrum pūcalum nikaḻum amarkkaḷamāyiruntatu,
avarkaḷiyaṭakki, araciṟkuriya amiti peṟuvittatōṭu. avar tam nāṭṭil amitiyum
iṉpamum nilavuvittaṉar. tam camayatti nāṭṭil parapputal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu ivartam
maṉattil mikkiruppiṉum, aracu kaṭṭil ēṟi makkaṭku nalam ceyvatum ciṟappuṭip
paṇiyākaliṉ, ivarkaḷ attuṟiyiṉum cīriya aṟamē purintu pōntaṉar. annāṭṭil,
perumpālum oru neṟippaṭṭa āṭcimuṟiyum, potu moḻiyum nilavacceyta ipperumakkaḷiṉ
tiruppaṇiyāl, iṭantōṟum moḻi, uṭi mutaliyavaṟṟil vēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭanta intiya makkaḷ,
tava camūkavoḻukkam, uṭi mutaliya avaṟṟuḷḷum orumi neṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. nāṭṭil
amitiyum iṉpamum nilavavē, makkaḷ tam tāymoḻiyilum mikka payiṟcikoṇṭu ciṟanta
ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar. iṉpamum, amitiyum urimiyum nilavum nāṭṭiltāṉ uyarnta
ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, ariya karuttukkaḷum piṟakkum eṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kālattil ivi
mikap peruki nāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappu nalkiṉa. tāṉ aṭinta mutumiyiṉit tāṉpeṟṟa
makaṉukku nalki, avaṉatu kaṭṭiḷamiyit tāṉ peṟṟu makiḻnta aracarkaḷ intukkaḷil
iruntaṉar. tam varalāṟṟiṉit tāmē varinta aracar pāpar perumāṉ. nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya
nāṇayaṅkaḷ, cakaṭakkāl pōl makkaḷ kiyakattu nillātu peyarum nīrmiyavāyiṉum, avarkaḷāl
kātalittup pōṟṟappaṭum iyalpaṟintu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka aṟattip parappi niṟuviya
aṟivunalam ivarkaḷpāl iyalpākavē amintu kiṭantatu. ‘nallatu ceyvatu āṇṭavaṉukku
aḷaviṟanta uvappit taruvatu; naṭunili tiṟampātu naṉṉeṟiyiṟ ceṉṟōr keṭuvatilar;
keṭṭār eṉa yām kēṭṭatumilli” eṉap poṟitta nāṇayaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ kālattil aṟaneṟi vaḻaṅki
makkaḷi aṟivuṭiyarākkiṉa. “ayiṉ akpari” eṉṟa araciyal nūl akpar eṉṉum aracar
peruntaki kālattil piṟantateṉpatu ciṟumakārum aṟinta ceyti yākum.
pōrttuṟiyil palatiṟamāṉapayiṟcikaḷum, nākarikattil ciṟappum, kalviyil perumāṇpum
nalkiya ivartam kālattil, kittoḻilum, ciṟpamum piṟavum ulaku pukaḻ uyarvu peṟṟaṉa.
intiya cīṉakkivēli yamippukkalanta intucāracaṉ kit tiṟaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḷam peṟṟaṉa. putiya
putiya ciṟpat toḻilkaḷ aracar ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. "puki mukantaṉṉa mācil tūvuṭi" eṉavum,
"nōkku nuḻikallā nuṇmiyapūk kaṉintu, aravuriyaṉṉa aṟuvi eṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻc
cāṉṟōr ceytutaviyatu pōṉṟa nuṇ viṉiyuṭikaḷum, melliya paṭṭāṭikaḷum ivarkaḷ kālattē
vaḷampeṟṟu ulaku pukaḻum oṇporuḷāyc ciṟantaṉa. kampaḷikaḷum, piṉṉalkaḷum, kiṉkāp
(Kin Khab) eṉpaṉavum, “nurikavarntaṉṉa meṉpūṅkampala"ṅkaḷum pukaḻvōr pulavariyiṟantu
viḷaṅkiṉa.
inta iculām camayap perumakkaḷāl nam nāṭṭil viḷinta nalaṅkaḷ palavum eḷitil kaṇṭu
kōṭaṟkeṉa oruvāṟu tiraṭṭit tantu ikkaṭṭuriyiṉi muṭittuk koḷḷukiṉṟām.
1. cōḻa vēntarkaḷiṉ vīḻccikkup piṉ keṭṭaḻintirunta kaṭaṟpaṭiyum piṟanāṭṭuk
kūṭṭuṟavum paṇṭēpōl mīṭṭum tōṟṟam peṟṟuc ciṟantaṉa.
2. vintiyavarikku vaṭakkil uḷḷa nāṭṭil, ivartam viṉittiṭpam, āṭci neṟi, naṉmaṉap
paṇpu eṉpavaṟṟāl amitiyum iṉpamum nilavuvavāyiṉa.
3. tām veṉṟu aṭippaṭutti nāṭeṅkum oru taṉmiyum, oruneṟiyum poruntiya
āṭcimuṟi ivarkaḷ kālattē nāṭṭil naṭipeṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
5. uyar vakuppiṉaruḷ camaya vēṟṟumiyiruntateṉiṉum, camūkavoḻukkam, uṭi mutaliyavaṟṟil
makkaṭkiṭiyē oṟṟumi yeyti yiruntatu. camaya vēṟṟumiyum kāyccalum pūcalumāy
māṟiyatu pēraracap peruntakikaḷ, kālattil illi,
5. intuttāṉiyeṉṟum, rekṭā eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum moḻi potumoḻiyāyiṟṟu; araciyaṟ
potumoḻi pāracika moḻi.
6. avvannāṭṭut tāymoḻikaḷ, amitiyum iṉpamum nilaviyataṉ payaṉāy vaḷam peṟṟaṉa.
varalāṟu kūṟum nūlkaḷum, araciyal nūlkaḷum nalliṭam peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.
7. pala teyvak koḷkiyum vaḻipāṭum kūṟum camaya neṟikaṭkiṭiyē oru teyvak koḷkiyum
vaḻipāṭum aṟiñar maṉattuḷ vērūṉṟi viḷaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷ cīriya tiruttam
aṭintaṉa.
8. pōrmuṟiyilum, nākarika vaḷarcciyilum palvaki nalaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷāl nam nāṭu peṟṟuc
ciṟappaṭintatu.
9. intucāracaṉ kittiṟaṅkaḷum, ciṟpat tiṟaṅkaḷum perumakkaḷiṉ ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. poṉṉum
maṇiyum ceṟinta nuṇmāṇkiviṉikaḷ, ivarkaḷ kālattiṟ peṟṟatu pōl piṟa ekkālattum
mēmpāṭu peṟavilliyeṉiṉ, aḵtu iṟappap pukaḻum pukaḻcciyākātu.
10. paṭṭiṉum paruttiyiṉum āya uṭivakikaḷ neytalum, kampala necavum piṟavum inta
islām camayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭil ceyta iṉiya toṇṭiṉāl mēṉmiyaṭintaṉa.
ikkūṟiya nalaṅkaḷ aṉittiṟkum uriyarāy viḷaṅkum ipperumakkaḷiṉ naṟpukaḻ innilavulakil
eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuka eṉa vāḻtti ikkaṭṭuriyi ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟōm.
-------

20. viññāṉamum tattuvañāṉamum

---
The Unity of Philosophical Experience by E. Gilson, P.III. pp.223–95.
tūttukkuṭi civacittānta capit talimiyuriyiṉ oru pakuti.
-------
ikkālam nuṇukik kāṇum viññāṉattukkuc ciṟappaḷittu ataṉāṟ peṟalākum aṟiviyal
nalaṅkaṭku mutaṉmi tantu nilavum kālamākum. viññāṉattāl viḷakkap paṭum poruḷkaḷē
uṇmi yeṉavum, koḷḷattakuvaṉa eṉavum karutuvatu ikkālattil ciṟappiyalpu. civamākiya
kaṭavuṭ poruḷ viññāṉa muṟiyil kāṇappaṭṭāloḻiyak koḷḷat- takuvataṉṟeṉavum ikkālam
karutuvatuṇṭu. aṟiveṉpatukūṭa viññāṉa muṟiyiṟ peṟuvatutāṉ ciṟappu eṉṟu
ikkālattavar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaṭku muṉṉaṇiyil niṟpavar imāṉuvēl kāṇṭu (Immanuel
Kant) eṉpārum, akastē kāntē (Augustine Conte) eṉpavarumāvar. ivviruvarum
tattaṅkoḷki vakiyil peritum vēṟupāṭu uṭiyavarāyiṉum "aṟivāvatu viññāṉa muṟiyiṟ
peṟappaṭum aṟivē” eṉṟa vakiyil ottuḷḷaṉar. viññāṉa muṟi aṟivāvatu niyūṭṭaṉ
(Newton) kaṇṭa aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi aṟiyavēṇṭiyavaṟṟi aṟivatākum. ulakiyaṟ poruḷoṉṟi
viññāṉa muṟiyil aṟivateṉpatu, kuṟitta poruḷiṉpāl piṟa poruṭkuḷḷa toṭarpukaḷik
kaṇakkiyal vāypāṭṭāṟ kaṇṭaṟivateṉpar.
civamākiya cemporuḷ ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka vittu viññāṉa muṟiyiṟ
kāṇakkūṭiyatu aṉmiyiṉ accem poruḷ aṟiyak kūṭiyataṉṟu; ākavē, ataṉippaṟṟip
pēcuvatu vīṇ ceyal eṉpatu avar koḷki. āyiṉum, ulakiyal nikaḻccikku attakiya
cemporuḷoṉṟu uḷatāka vittuc ceyvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu eṉavum, eṉavē, atu
nammāl vakuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa toṉṟē tavira, aṟiviyal ārāycciyāl aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya
oṉṟaṉṟu eṉavum kāṇṭu (Kant) eṉpavar kūṟukiṉṟār. kāntē (Conte) eṉpār vēṟu
vakiyil tamatu nāvaṉmiyic celutti inta muṭivukkē vantucērntār.
ivartam koḷkikaḷuḷ kāṇṭeṉpārip piṉpaṟṟi jermaṉi nāṭṭil pālceṉ (Paulsen)
viciṅkar (Vaishinger) mutaliyōrum, pireñcu nāṭṭil, riṉōviyar (Removier)
mutaliyōrum, amerikkāvil liyōṉ piraṉcik (Prof Leon Brunschvieg)
mutaliyōrum, kāntēyip piṉpaṟṟi iṅkilāntil jāṉ sṭuvarṭmil (John Stuart Mill)
herparṭ speṉcar (Herbart Spencer) mutaliyōrum, pireñcu nāṭṭil emil litrē
("Emile Littre") emil ṭarkkīm ("Emile Durkheim") mutaliyōrum nāṭṭiṟ
parappaluṟṟaṉar.
putumiyil makkaṭku viḻivum viyappum uṇṭātal iyalpu. viññāṉikaḷ iṅṅaṉam putu
muṟiyil tamatu pēccu vaṉmiyum eḻuttu vaṉmiyum kūṭṭik kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyit tākkat
toṭaṅkiyatum, viññāṉam kāṭṭum putumikaḷ kaṇṭu viyappāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭukkiṭanta
makkaḷatu uḷḷam nilikalaṅkiṟṟu. viññāṉat tuṟiyiṉ nōkkatti nuṇukik kāṇum tiṟam
iḻantavar. avar koḷkikaṭku iriyāyiṉar. ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ ākkak kūṟukaḷum, avi,
tammil iyintu amintirukkum amitiyum, avaṟṟiṉ ūṭē nilavum oḻuṅkum kaṇṭu eppaṭi
yamintuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟivatē viññāṉattiṉ muḻutta nōkkamākum. poruḷkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam,
nili, kēṭu ākiyaṉa uṇṭātal eṅṅaṉam eṉṟu kaṇṭu, avi ēṉ aṅṅaṉamāyiṉa eṉṟu kāṇātu
pōtalāl, viññāṉattāṟpeṟum ulakiyalaṟivu muḻutta aṟivākāteṉpati makkaḷ aṟintilar.
poruḷkaḷiṉ ākka amitikaḷi ārāyuṅkāl putiya putiya aṟivum avvaḻi avaṟṟāl uṇṭākum
iṉpamum avaratu ārāycciyaṟivi maḻuppi viṭuvatāl, viññāṉa aṟivu orumaruṅku
paṟṟiyatāka iruttali viññāṉikaḷ tāmum aṟiyātoḻikiṉṟaṉar. mēlum avarkaṭkuk
kaṭavuḷākiya cemporuḷ viññāṉa neṟiyil ārāyattakka poruḷāka illi. viññāṉa neṟiyiṟ
kāṇappaṭum poruḷiṉkaṇ ūṭuruvi niṟkum iyaṟkiyē muḻumutalākiṟatu. ataṉik kāṭṭum
poruḷkaḷiṉ ākka amitikaṭku uriya kāraṇatti (ākkamum amitiyum ēṉ uṇṭāyiṉa eṉak
kāṇum kāṭciyāl aṟitaṟkeḻum uriya kāraṇatti) nōkkum niliyil nām iruttalāl avar
karutti ēṟkiṉṟōmilli. nīr eppaṭi yuṇṭākiṟateṉa viññāṉattik kēṭpiṉ, aḵtu iru
kūṟu nīrkkāṟṟum, oru kūṟu neruppukkāṟṟum kūṭi nīruṇṭākiṟatu eṉṟum; ivvāṟu
ēṉ uṇṭāka vēṇṭum eṉṟāl, viññāṉam viṭi tarātu.
poruḷkaḷiṉ ākka amitikaṇṭu ēṉ eṉṉātu eppaṭiyeṉa viṉāvic cēral viññāṉattiṉ
kuṟippu. eppaṭi yeṉṉātu ēṉ eṉa viṉāvic celvatu tattuva ñāṉattiṉ kuṟippākum.
itaṉi mēṉāṭṭār meyyiyal (Philosophy) eṉpar. viññāṉa tattuva ñāṉamum tattamakkuriya
ellik kaṇ niṟkumāyiṉ, uṇmi yaṟivu viḷakkamuṟum, ivviru ñāṉaṅkaḷum tammuḷ
māṟukoṇṭu oṉṟaṉiyoṉṟu keṭuttaṟkē muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. ulaka varalāṟu kāṇpōr, tattuva
ñāṉattiṉ celvākkāl viññāṉikaḷ palar peruṅkēṭṭuk kuḷḷākiyatik kāṇpar. viññāṉikaḷ
palar nāṭu kaṭattappaṭṭatum, uyirkkoli ceyyappaṭṭatum varalāṟṟu nūl
vāyviṭṭaraṟṟukiṟatu. kāla vēṟupāṭṭāl tattuvañāṉam celvākkuk kuṟiyaluṟṟatum,
viññāṉam celvākkuṟṟut tattuvañāṉa ellikkuḷ pukuvatāyiṟṟu, taṉṉellikkuṭ paṭāta
cemporuḷi viññāṉam tēṭiyārāyntu “aṟputamō civaṉaruḷō aṟiyēṉ” eṉa alamarukiṟatu.
tattuva ñāṉamum, viññāṉamum vēṟu vēṟu kuṟippiṉa vāyiṉum, tattuva ñāṉattukku
viññāṉamum iṉṟi yamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum. uṇmi vaḻiniṉṟu kaṭavuḷuṇmi kāṇum neṟi,
tattuva ñāṉa neṟiyiṉ vayappaṭṭu iyaluvatu. ātalāl, tattuva ñāṉiyoruvar oṉṟaṉippaṟṟip
pēca varumuṉ atu paṟṟi viññāṉi yātu kūṟukiṉṟār eṉpati mutaṟkaṇ kāṇṭal vēṇṭum
eṉpar. oru poruḷiṉ ākkam eṅṅaṉa māyiṟṟeṉpatu kaṇṭāl aḵtu aṅṅaṉam ēṉ āyiṟṟeṉak
kāṇumāṟṟāl uṇmit taṉmi yuṇāl kūṭum eṉṟu car. jēms jīṉs (Sir James Jeans)
eṉpār kūṟukiṉṟār. viññāṉak kāṭciyiṟ cellumiṭattu mikka viḻippōṭu
cellavēṇṭum. ciṟitu ayarntaviṭattu, viññāṉam tattuvañāṉa ellikkuṭ pukuntu
mayakkattiṟ celuttiviṭum. piṉṉar, tattuva ñāṉa neṟiyiṟ kāṇum poruḷkaṭku viññāṉa
neṟiyil viḷakkaṅ kāṇac celutti uḻalvikkum viññāṉamum tattuva ñāṉamum
oruṅkuṭiyarākiya car. jēms jīṉs 1930-ām āṇṭil, tattuvañāṉap poruṭku viññāṉa
viḷakkaṅ kūṟat talippaṭṭu muṭivil “aṟputa pirapañcam" (The Mysterious Universe)
eṉṟa nūli eḻuti muṭittār.
ippirapañcam aṟputamāṉatu eṉṟataṟku oru viññāṉi vēṇṭumā? makkaḷ nākarikamiṉṟi
vilaṅku pōl vāḻnta kālattēyē ivvulakam aṟputamāṉatu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭṭatu.
viññāṉattiṉ karuttu pirapañcattiṉ aṟputat taṉmiyi māṟṟi viḷaṅkavippatākum.
attuṟiyil muyaṉṟu aḵtu evvaḷavō viḷakkattit tantirukkiṟataṉṟō innāḷil paḷḷiyil
payilum oru māṇavaṉ tāmas ākviṉās, aristāttal, piḷēṭō mutalāyiṉōr aṟiyāta uṇmi
kaḷi naṉku aṟintuḷḷāṉ. avarkaṭkup perumayakkattit tanta ulaka nikaḻccikaṭku ikkāla
viññāṉam teḷivuṭiya viḷakkan tantuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamirukka, viññāṉikaḷ ivvulakam
aṟputamāṉa teṉpārāyiṉ, avar kūṟṟu naṉku cintikkattakkatoṉṟām. cintittaviṭattu
uṇmi pulaṉākiṟatu. tattuvaneṟiyiṟ kāṇpaṉavaṟṟi viññāṉamākak karuti ataṟku viññāṉa
muṟiyil viḷakkaṅ kūṟappukuvatē avar ceyyum tavaṟu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ ulakam aṟputamāṉatu
eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭiyavarāyiṉar. tattuva ñāṉak kāṭcip poruḷ viññāṉak kāṭcikku
akappaṭāmiyiṉ, viññāṉam ataṉik kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇi ceyyātāyiṟṟu eṉpatē avar kūṟuvataṉ
karuttākum. mēlum, viññāṉa muṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum koḷkikaḷ oru nilimiyaṉavalla.
kālañcellac cellap putukkāṭcikaḷāl māṟupaṭuvaṉa. "kuṟipāṭu uḷḷataṟkaṉṟē
vaḷarcciyuṇṭāvatu" eṉpar tirukkōviyārukku urikaṇṭa pērāciriyar. mēṉmēlum vaḷarntu
ciṟattalāl kuṟipāṭuṭiya viññāṉam, oru muṭivu kaṇṭu muṟṟi niṟkum tattuvañāṉattōṭu
iyiyātākaliṉ pirapañcattiṉ aṟputa nilimiyi viḷakkātāyiṟṟu.
"vantavāṟu eṅṅaṉē pōmāṟētō, māyamāmperu vāḻvu" eṉṟār nam nāvaracar. itaṉ
mutaṟpakutikku urikūṟuvōrpōla, makkaḷuyir ulakiṟku vantavāṟṟik kūṟaluṟṟu, viṇmīṉ
vīḻcciyum, ñāyiṟṟut teṟippum, nilavulakiṉ tōṟṟamum piṟavum viriyakkūṟi
"innilavulakiṟku makkaḷuyir vantatutaṟceyalāka amintatu eṉa viññāṉam
kūṟukiṟatu”[1]. * eṉṟu car jēms jīṉs urittār. taṟceyal (Accident) viḷakkamillāta
collātaliṉ, "vantavāṟu" kāṇṭaṟku viññāṉam valiyiḻantu niṟkiṟateṉpatu teḷivākum.
piṉpu avar, aiṉsṭaṉ (Einstein) hicaṉpark (Heisenberg) tirā ak (Dirac) @av
Guogi (Lematre) lūyi-te-purēkḷi (Louis-de-Broglie) mutalāyiṉar kaṇṭa peḷatika
vulaki viḷakkik kūṟi, muṭivil tattuva ñāṉameṉṟa āḻkaṭaluḷ mūḻkukiṉṟār. ataṉkaṇ
pirapañcam ‘kaṭacakkarar entiremaṉac cuḻalvatu’[2] eṉak kāṇkiṉṟār. muṭivil, itu
‘paṭikkappaṭṭatē’ eṉṟu tēṟi, ituvum caṅkaṟpattāl paṭikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉavum[3] itaṉip
paṭittōṉ, oviyam vallāṉoruvaṉ tāṉ tīṭṭum ōviyakkiḻikkup puṟampē niṉṟu oviyatti
eḻutuvatupōla, kālam, iṭam mutaliyaṉa kaṭantu niṉṟē paṭittiruttal vēṇṭum eṉa iṉṟi
viññāṉak koḷki nammi niṉikkumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu eṉṟa karuttuppaṭavum kūṟi
muṭikkiṉṟār.
pirapañcavaṭṭameṉavum, caṅkaṟpaciruṭṭiyeṉavum ivar kūṟuvaṉa mēṉāṭṭuc cākraṭīcu mutaliya
tattuva ñāṉikaṭkum nam nāṭṭup peḷakkara meḻutiya puṭkarar mutaliya civañāṉikaṭkum[4]
muṉṉirunta muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭaṉavākum. ivi pala nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē nam muṉṉōr kaṇṭu
urittiruppa vum, cuṟṟippōyum cuṅkaccāvaṭikkē vantatu pōla, putiya putiyavāka
viññāṉa neṟiyil nuṇukic ceṉṟu nuṇukkariya nuṇṇuṇarvu kāṇum aiṉsṭaṉ mutalāyiṉōr
inta muṭiviyē kaṇṭurikkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatu car jēms jīṉs kūṟuvataṉāl viḷaṅkukiṟataṉṟō?

iṉi, jūliyaṉ haksili (Julian Huxley) eṉpār ulakil uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa varalāṟṟi
yārāyntu kūṟic cellum kūṟṟukkaḷil A.H. kāmpṭaṉ (A.H. Compton)
kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟilum, viññāṉamum, tattuvañāṉamum tammuḷ mayaṅkip palataliyāṉa muṭivukaṇṭu
alamaruvatu naṉku pulaṉākum. aṉṟiyum, aṇuvippaṟṟiya ārāycci tōṉṟi avarkaḷatu
mayakkatti mikutippaṭuttukiṟatu. peḷatika racāyaṉa aṇukkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl uṇṭākum
poruḷkaḷiṉiṭiyē uyiraṇukkaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. inta uyiraṇukkaḷ eṅṅaṉa muṇṭāyiṉa eṉak
kāṇum viññāṉikaḷ perumayakkamuṟṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. itukāṟum oru viññāṉiyum uyiraṇu
voṉṟip paṭittatu kiṭiyātu. uyiraṇuveṉpatu peḷatika aṇuvōṭu kūṭiyē yirukkiṟatu.
avvaṇuviṉ peḷatikap pakutikku vēṟāka yātum ataṉkaṇ itukāṟum kāṇappaṭa villi.
peḷatikappakutiyiṉ vēṟāka uyirt taṉmi, ataṉpāl eṅṅaṉa muṇṭāyiṟṟeṉa ārāynta
viññāṉikaḷ oru muṭivum kāṇātu mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum, aṇut tiraḷkaḷiṉ iṭiyē,
purōṭāṉs (Protons), elakṭrāṉs (Electrons), nīyūṭrāṉs, (Newtrons), ḵpōṭṭāṉs
(Photons) eṉṟa aṇukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku viññāṉa muṟiyil viḷakkaṅ kāṇa
muṭiyavilli. ivvāṟu viññāṉam kāṭṭum kāṭcikaḷ pala namatu uṇmikkē viḷakkam
pulappaṭuttāmal uḷḷaṉa. iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟāl mayakkamum, tattuvañāṉamum paṇṭi nāḷil ceyta
tavaṟupaṟṟi ataṉpāl kāḻppum koṇṭu, kaṭavuḷākiya cemporuḷuṇmi kaṇṭu mēṟkoḷḷātu,
Blind Evolution eṉavum, Clear sighted Orthogenesis eṉavum piṟavum cila
viññāṉikaḷ kūṟuvatu paṟṟi kūṟuvatupaṟṟi, kaṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷ mayaṅki yoḻital kūṭātu.
